**Report returned**

````text
Follow-up report: Vercel external-rewrite proxy in front of a Laravel + Sanctum API on Render (checked 2026-11-16)

Headline: the routing, caching, timeout and secret-header parts are documented and I could pin them down. Cookie pass-through, the upstream Host header and the forwarding of ordinary request headers are **not documented by Vercel anywhere I could find**; the evidence there is indirect, so they sit in the live-test list. Statements are paraphrased from the linked sources; UNCONFIRMED and INFERENCE are marked. No files written.

---

## 1. Cookies through an external rewrite

- **No official statement found.** The rewrites docs, the vercel.json reference, the reverse-proxy KB guide, the standalone-CDN KB guide and the request-flow page say nothing about the `Cookie` request header, `Set-Cookie` pass-through, multiple `Set-Cookie` headers, or rewriting of `Domain`/`Secure`/`SameSite`. UNCONFIRMED from primary sources.
  - https://vercel.com/docs/routing/rewrites
  - https://vercel.com/docs/project-configuration/vercel-json
  - https://vercel.com/kb/guide/vercel-reverse-proxy-rewrites-external
  - https://vercel.com/kb/guide/using_vercel_as_a_cdn
  - https://vercel.com/docs/fundamentals/infrastructure
- **Indirect official evidence that cookies do pass:**
  - The CDN cache criteria say a response is not cached if it contains `set-cookie`, which presupposes such responses are passed through. https://vercel.com/docs/caching/cdn-cache#cacheable-response-criteria
  - `has`/`missing` rewrite conditions can match on `cookie`, so the request cookie is visible at the routing layer. https://vercel.com/docs/project-configuration/vercel-json
  - Vercel runs its own community forum (Discourse, a cookie-session app) behind a single external rewrite plus a secret header. The post does not discuss cookies explicitly. https://vercel.com/blog/how-we-run-vercels-cdn-in-front-of-discourse (March 10, 2026)
- **Nothing found saying Vercel alters cookie attributes.** No source describes Domain/Secure/SameSite being rewritten or stripped. UNCONFIRMED either way. Practical consequence: the upstream must not emit a `Domain` attribute naming the onrender.com host; your host-only cookie is the right shape.
- **Reported failures and their causes:**
  - Next.js issue #29488 claimed `Set-Cookie` was dropped through a rewrite; the author later reported it was working and closed it, with no root cause and no maintainer reply. https://github.com/vercel/next.js/issues/29488
  - Next.js discussion #36987: a user could not stop cookies being forwarded on an external rewrite, i.e. the request `Cookie` header goes upstream as-is (user report, not staff). https://github.com/vercel/next.js/discussions/36987
  - edge-runtime issue #536: several `Set-Cookie` headers merged into one comma-separated header. That concerns `fetch` inside edge functions, not vercel.json rewrites. https://github.com/vercel/edge-runtime/issues/536
  - The common "cookie lost" reports in search results turned out to be third-party cookie blocking, fixed by adding exactly this kind of rewrite (third-party repos, anecdotal only).

## 2. Request headers, Host, size limits

- **Documented as sent to the external origin:** `x-real-ip`, `x-forwarded-for` (visitor IP), `x-forwarded-host` (host the visitor requested), `x-forwarded-proto`. https://vercel.com/docs/routing/rewrites#identifying-the-visitor-at-your-origin
- Vercel overwrites `X-Forwarded-For` rather than forwarding a client-supplied value. https://vercel.com/docs/headers/request-headers
- **Origin, Referer, Sec-Fetch-Site, Accept, Content-Type, X-XSRF-TOKEN, custom headers:** no official list of what is forwarded unchanged. UNCONFIRMED. Indirect support: the docs offer a transform to *delete* or *set* request headers on a route, which implies headers are otherwise forwarded. https://vercel.com/docs/project-configuration/vercel-json (Transform examples)
- **Host header received upstream:** UNCONFIRMED in docs. One open, unanswered bug report (Next.js middleware rewrite between two Vercel apps) observed `host` equal to the destination host. https://github.com/vercel/next.js/issues/67469 . INFERENCE: destination host, which Render needs to route to the service.
- **Size limits (documented):**
  - Any single request header, name included, max 32 KB; all cookies travel in one `Cookie` header so they share that 32 KB. https://vercel.com/docs/errors/REQUEST_HEADER_TOO_LARGE
  - URL max 14 KB. https://vercel.com/docs/errors/URL_TOO_LONG
  - Body size for proxied requests: nothing documented. The 4.5 MB limit is stated for Vercel Functions only. https://vercel.com/docs/errors/FUNCTION_PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE . UNCONFIRMED whether any cap applies to external rewrites.

## 3. vercel.json for this case

**Ordering and filesystem precedence**
- The filesystem is checked before `rewrites` are applied, so built files (`/assets/*.js`, `/favicon.ico`) are served directly and never hit the fallback. https://vercel.com/docs/project-configuration/vercel-json (rewrites)
- Source confirms the compile order: user `routes`, then redirects, then headers, then a filesystem handler, then `rewrites` in array order. https://github.com/vercel/vercel/blob/main/packages/routing-utils/src/index.ts
- Vercel's own SPA example is a single `/(.*)` to `/index.html` rewrite. https://vercel.com/docs/frameworks/frontend/vite#using-vite-to-make-spas
- Recommended form, API rule first and the fallback excluding `/api` so ordering cannot matter:

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://openapi.vercel.sh/vercel.json",
  "rewrites": [
    { "source": "/api/:path*", "destination": "https://<service>.onrender.com/api/:path*" },
    { "source": "/((?!api/).*)", "destination": "/index.html" }
  ],
  "headers": [
    {
      "source": "/api/:path*",
      "headers": [{ "key": "x-vercel-enable-rewrite-caching", "value": "0" }]
    }
  ]
}
```

- Side effect (INFERENCE): a request for a missing asset, such as a stale hashed chunk after a redeploy, returns `index.html` with 200 instead of 404.
- Not asked, but it will bite: Sanctum registers its CSRF route at `/sanctum/csrf-cookie` (prefix from `sanctum.prefix`), outside `/api`. With only `/api` proxied, set that prefix to `api/sanctum` or add a second rewrite, and keep login/logout under `/api`. https://github.com/laravel/sanctum/blob/4.x/src/SanctumServiceProvider.php

**Root Directory**
- The docs say vercel.json belongs in the project's root directory, and the monorepo docs show per-app files such as `apps/web/vercel.json`. So `web/vercel.json` is the one read when Root Directory is `web`. https://vercel.com/docs/project-configuration/vercel-json , https://vercel.com/docs/monorepos
- I did not find a sentence stating outright that a repo-root vercel.json is ignored when Root Directory is set; treat that as implied.
- Root Directory also sets the install path; by default the build can still reach files outside it. https://vercel.com/docs/builds/configure-a-build#root-directory

**Vite preset defaults**
- Detected when `vite` is a package dependency. Build command `vite build` (dashboard placeholder: `npm run build` or `vite build`), output directory `dist`, env prefix `VITE_`. https://github.com/vercel/vercel/blob/main/packages/frameworks/src/frameworks.ts
- The build-settings page says the build command can defer to the `build` script in package.json (shown for Next.js; not stated specifically for Vite). https://vercel.com/docs/builds/configure-a-build

## 4. CDN caching of proxied responses

- The opt-out is a **vercel.json `headers` rule on the source path**, not a header from the origin: key `x-vercel-enable-rewrite-caching`, value `0`, on `/api/:path*` (as in the config above). Value `1` is the opt-in for older projects. https://vercel.com/docs/routing/rewrites#disabling-caching-for-rewrites-to-external-origins
- Default-on applies to projects created on or after April 6, 2026. https://vercel.com/changelog/vercels-cdn-now-respects-cache-control-headers-from-external-origins-by-default
- Laravel's default is confirmed: Symfony's `ResponseHeaderBag` emits `no-cache, private` when no Cache-Control is set (and `private, must-revalidate` if `Last-Modified` or `Expires` is present). https://github.com/symfony/symfony/blob/7.4/src/Symfony/Component/HttpFoundation/ResponseHeaderBag.php
- That already blocks caching: Vercel will not cache a response with `private`, `no-cache` or `no-store`, nor one carrying `set-cookie`, nor a non-GET/HEAD request. Your API responses meet at least two of these. https://vercel.com/docs/caching/cdn-cache#cacheable-response-criteria
- So the opt-out header is belt and braces. Keep it, in case a route ever sets a public Cache-Control.

## 5. Render's side

- All inbound traffic goes through Cloudflare and then Render's load balancers; apps see a proxy IP on the socket and should read `x-forwarded-for`; `CF-Ray` is forwarded. https://render.com/articles/how-render-handles-ddos-attacks (April 1, 2026)
- `X-Forwarded-For` is **appended to, not overwritten**, so the leftmost entry is caller-controlled on a public service; `CF-Connecting-IP` is overwritten by Cloudflare on every request. https://render.com/articles/host-pocketbase-on-render
  - A Render community thread reportedly says the same about appending; I only saw it in a search summary because the forum redirect would not load. https://community.render.com/t/forward-real-ip-when-rewrite-urls/933
  - INFERENCE for this chain: Laravel sees `X-Forwarded-For` as visitor IP (set by Vercel), then Vercel's egress IP, and `CF-Connecting-IP` is Vercel's egress IP, not the visitor.
- `X-Forwarded-Proto`, `X-Forwarded-Host` and `Host` handling: not documented by Render. UNCONFIRMED. Render's load balancer terminates TLS and forwards to the service's port (default 10000). https://render.com/docs/web-services
- **Request timeout:** Render web services allow HTTP responses to take up to 100 minutes. https://render.com/docs/render-vs-vercel-comparison . The binding limit in this chain is therefore Vercel's 120 seconds. https://vercel.com/docs/limits#proxied-request-timeout
- Free tier: spins down after 15 minutes idle, about a minute to wake, and Render shows a loading page to connecting browsers; behaviour for a proxied XHR is undocumented. https://render.com/docs/free

## 6. Secret header to the origin on Hobby

- Yes in plain vercel.json, and no plan restriction is stated for `routes` transforms or `env` expansion. UNCONFIRMED only in the sense that the docs are silent on plans rather than naming Hobby. https://vercel.com/docs/routing/rewrites#restricting-your-origin-to-vercel-traffic , https://vercel.com/docs/project-configuration/vercel-json (Transform object definition)
- Documented pattern: a `routes` entry with an external `dest` and a `request.headers` / `set` transform whose `args` is `$ORIGIN_SECRET` with `env: ["ORIGIN_SECRET"]`. `set` replaces any visitor-supplied value.
- `routes` may now be used alongside `rewrites` and `headers` (docs), and user routes run before the filesystem check and rewrites (source). Combined config for this case, assembled from those documented pieces:

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://openapi.vercel.sh/vercel.json",
  "routes": [
    {
      "src": "/api/(.*)",
      "dest": "https://<service>.onrender.com/api/$1",
      "transforms": [
        {
          "type": "request.headers",
          "op": "set",
          "target": { "key": "x-origin-secret" },
          "args": "$ORIGIN_SECRET",
          "env": ["ORIGIN_SECRET"]
        }
      ]
    }
  ],
  "rewrites": [
    { "source": "/((?!api/).*)", "destination": "/index.html" }
  ],
  "headers": [
    {
      "source": "/api/:path*",
      "headers": [{ "key": "x-vercel-enable-rewrite-caching", "value": "0" }]
    }
  ]
}
```

- Gotchas from the docs:
  - If the env var is not set in the project, the literal string `$ORIGIN_SECRET` is sent. Set it for every environment you deploy (Production and Preview).
  - Changing the variable needs a redeploy; rotate by accepting old and new values at the origin.
  - With `routes` plus `rewrites`/`headers` in one file, `src` matching is case-sensitive.
- Origin side: reject requests whose header does not match, using a timing-safe comparison, and exempt Render's health-check path (INFERENCE: health checks do not come through Vercel).

---

## Things only a live test can settle

1. Both `Set-Cookie` headers (session and `XSRF-TOKEN`) arrive at the browser as two separate headers with `Secure`, `HttpOnly`, `SameSite=Lax` and no `Domain` intact. Check in Safari and Chrome Incognito.
2. The browser's `Cookie` header reaches Laravel on the next proxied request.
3. `Origin`, `Referer`, `Sec-Fetch-Site`, `Accept`, `Content-Type` and `X-XSRF-TOKEN` reach Laravel unchanged. Sanctum's stateful check depends on Referer/Origin, and Laravel 13's CSRF check on `Sec-Fetch-Site`.
4. Which `Host` Laravel sees, and whether Vercel's `x-forwarded-host` survives Cloudflare and Render's load balancer (dump headers from a temporary debug route).
5. What `X-Forwarded-For` and `X-Forwarded-Proto` look like after the full chain, and what `$request->ip()` and `$request->isSecure()` return with `trustProxies(at: '*')`.
6. What the SPA receives when the Render service is asleep: a held request that succeeds within 120s, Render's HTML loading page, or a 502 `ROUTER_EXTERNAL_TARGET_ERROR`.
7. That the `routes` + `rewrites` + `headers` combination deploys cleanly and behaves as intended, including the secret header arriving with the expanded value.
8. That `x-vercel-cache` shows no HIT on any `/api` response.
9. That `web/vercel.json` is picked up with Root Directory `web` (visible immediately: `/api/...` proxies and deep links load).
10. Whether any body-size cap applies to proxied uploads, if the API accepts files.
````

### Frontend consistency and accessibility review

**Prompt written by the assistant**

```text
Read-only release review of a React 19 + TypeScript + Vite + Tailwind 4 + TanStack Query single-page app at /Users/[redacted: account name]/Desktop/eneba-marketplace/web (an Eneba-style marketplace for digital game keys, internship assignment). DO NOT modify, create or delete any file, do not run git commands, do not start servers, do not read `.env` files. You may run `npx tsc --noEmit -p .` or `npm run lint` inside web/ if useful, nothing else.

The pages were built in separate phases (account → store → product → search → cart/checkout/orders → homepage). Goal: find where that shows, and find real defects, before submission. I want an independent read. Report only what you can point to with file:line. For each finding give: severity (high / medium / low), the user-visible impact, and the smallest fix. No refactors for their own sake, no new features (no wishlists, reviews, dashboards).

Read: src/routes.tsx, src/main.tsx, src/index.css (design tokens), src/components/*, src/pages/*, src/lib/*, index.html, public/ (favicon, covers), vercel.json, vite.config.ts.

Questions:
1. Visual consistency across pages, judged from the class names: are page headings, section headings, body text sizes, container widths, page padding, card styles, button styles (primary/secondary/disabled/busy), form inputs, error/empty/loading states, price formatting and discount badges defined once and reused, or re-declared with different values per page? List concrete mismatches (e.g. "h1 is text-2xl font-black on X but text-3xl font-extrabold on Y"), grouped, most noticeable first.
2. Interaction states: every button and link — hover, focus-visible ring, disabled, pending. Any clickable div without role/keyboard support? Any focus outline removed without replacement?
3. Accessibility from the markup: one h1 per page and sane heading order, landmarks, skip link, labels on every input, `aria-live` for async results and errors, alt text policy for cover images, colour-only meaning, `lang`, document title per route (does the title change on navigation?), focus management on route change, reduced-motion handling, touch target sizes on mobile.
4. Routing: unknown route handling (404 page?), scroll restoration on navigation, protected routes when signed out (cart, orders, order detail), redirect-after-login safety, error boundary for render errors, behaviour when a lazy chunk or the API is unavailable.
5. Loading and error states: every `useQuery`/`useMutation` — is there a loading state, an error state with a retry, and an empty state? Any place a failed request renders nothing or spins forever? Layout shift between skeleton and content?
6. Images: width/height or aspect-ratio set, lazy loading below the fold, `onError` fallback, sizes appropriate.
7. Responsive: from the classes, anything likely to overflow or cramp at 375 px, 768 px and 1024 px (tables, the offer table, cart lines, header with search + nav + cart + account, filter panel).
8. Meta: index.html title, description, favicon, theme-color, Open Graph; any leftover Vite boilerplate, placeholder text, TODOs, console.log, dead components or unused exports, stale copy that says a feature is "coming later".
9. Security in the client: any `dangerouslySetInnerHTML`, unvalidated redirects, secrets in the bundle, license keys written to localStorage or logs.

Final output: a severity-ordered findings list under ~900 words, most user-visible first, each marked CONFIRMED (read in code) or PLAUSIBLE (needs a browser to confirm); then a short "checked, fine" list.
```

**Report returned**

```text
Release review of `/Users/[redacted: account name]/Desktop/eneba-marketplace/web` (all paths below are relative to that directory). Read-only; nothing changed. `npx tsc --noEmit -p .` and `npm run lint` are both clean. Where I say "built CSS" I read the existing `dist/assets/index-BS85PzGd.css`.

## Findings, most user-visible first

**HIGH**

1. **Signing out leaves orders and license keys on screen** — CONFIRMED. `src/lib/auth.ts:37,41` (and `src/pages/CartPage.tsx:64`) only set `['current-user']`; the `['orders']` and `['order', id]` caches survive.
   - `src/pages/OrdersPage.tsx:23` and `:50` are independent conditions, so after sign-out, pressing Back shows "Sign in to see your orders" with the previous account's list under it.
   - `src/pages/OrderPage.tsx:87` renders the cached order, keys included, until the 401 arrives; it stays up if that refetch fails with a 5xx.
   - Fix: in `useAuthAction`'s `onSuccess` and its 401 branch, add `queryClient.removeQueries({ queryKey: ['orders'] })` and the same for `['order']`.

**MEDIUM**

2. **No error boundary** — CONFIRMED. `src/routes.tsx:13-26` has no `errorElement`, so any render exception replaces the whole app, header included, with React Router's "Unexpected Application Error!" and a stack trace. Fix: wrap the children in one pathless route whose `errorElement` renders a `Notice`.

3. **Favicon is the Vite starter logo** — CONFIRMED. `public/favicon.svg` is the purple bolt; `index.html:5-7` has no description, theme-color or Open Graph tags. Fix: own SVG plus `description` and `theme-color` (`#4618ac`).

4. **Account page still looks like phase 1** — CONFIRMED.
   - `src/pages/AccountPage.tsx:24-25`: bare "Checking your session…" and "The API is not reachable." with no error colour, no `role`, no retry. `auth.ts:23` has `retry: false`, so one cold-start failure is a dead end, while every other page says "server may be starting up" with Try again.
   - `:84`: the submit button is disabled while pending but looks and reads the same.
   - `:80`: prints the raw `error.message` ("Failed to fetch", "Request failed with status 502").
   - `:107`: `current-password` autocomplete in register mode.
   - Fix: `Notice tone="error"` with a refetch button, a pending label, and a generic fallback message.

5. **Cart tells signed-in users to sign in** — CONFIRMED. `CartPage.tsx:84,138-149` treats a pending or failed `/auth/me` as signed out. Fix: show the disabled checkout button while `currentUser.isPending`. Also `:120` keeps saying "Checking prices and stock…" after the quote has failed.

6. **Button states are not in the shared constants** — CONFIRMED. `src/components/States.tsx:35-39` has no `disabled:` or `cursor-pointer`.
   - Tailwind 4 gives `<button>` the arrow cursor, so the same yellow button is a hand as a `Link` and an arrow as a button.
   - `disabled:opacity-60` is added ad hoc at `CartPage.tsx:139` and `OrderPage.tsx:237,240`, but missing at `CartPage.tsx:134` (Update cart), `:229` (Remove) and `AccountPage.tsx:42,84`.
   - Re-declared buttons: `OfferTable.tsx:65` (no fixed height), `FilterPanel.tsx:124` (h-9 copy of secondary), `SearchBox.tsx:156`, and `CartPage.tsx:199,211` (no hover).
   - Fix: add `cursor-pointer disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60` to both constants and reuse them in OfferTable and FilterPanel.

7. **Headings differ by phase** — CONFIRMED.
   - h1: `text-3xl font-bold` on `StorePage.tsx:53`, `CartPage.tsx:71,93`, `OrdersPage.tsx:19`, `AccountPage.tsx:22`; `text-2xl sm:text-[26px] font-bold` on `ProductPage.tsx:94`; `text-2xl font-bold` on `OrderPage.tsx:200,253,276`; home uses `font-black` (`HomeBlocks.tsx:28,66`, `HomePage.tsx:37`).
   - h2: `text-xl font-bold` (`ProductPage.tsx:152,160`), `text-lg font-extrabold` (`CartPage.tsx:118`, `OrderPage.tsx:103,296`), `text-xl font-extrabold` (`States.tsx:28`).
   - Top gap: `py-6` store, `pt-8` cart/orders/order, `py-4` product breadcrumb, `pt-14` loading (`ProductPage.tsx:187`, `OrderPage.tsx:46`).
   - Fix: two exported class constants (`pageTitle`, `sectionTitle`) and one top padding.

8. **Homepage jumps 16px on load** — CONFIRMED. `first:mt-6` at `HomeBlocks.tsx:23` never matches because the sr-only h1 (`HomePage.tsx:60`) is the first child. The hero sits at `mt-10` while the skeleton uses `pt-6` (`HomePage.tsx:72`). Fix: wrap the blocks in a `<div>`.

9. **Six full-page states have no h1** — CONFIRMED. `NotFoundPage.tsx:10`, `ProductPage.tsx:28,41`, `OrderPage.tsx:51,64,77`; `Notice` always renders an h2. Fix: a heading-level prop on `Notice`.

**LOW**

10. **Error and stale data render together** — PLAUSIBLE (needs the API asleep on tab refocus). `StorePage.tsx:142` with `:178`; `OrdersPage.tsx:32` with `:50`; `ProductPage.tsx:38` replaces a loaded product with the error. Fix: `&& !x.data`, as `CartPage.tsx:97` already does.

11. **Every filter tick or sort change scrolls to the top** — CONFIRMED in router code, feel is PLAUSIBLE. `StorePage.tsx:77,107` with `AppShell.tsx:59`. Fix: `setParams(next, { preventScrollReset: true })`. Separately, "Apply price" remounts its form (`FilterPanel.tsx:25`), dropping keyboard focus to `<body>`.

12. **Loading and filter-error states are uneven** — CONFIRMED. Skeletons on home, store and product; plain text on `OrdersPage.tsx:21`, `OrderPage.tsx:46`. A filters failure (`StorePage.tsx:76`) has no retry and removes the sort select (`:100`).

13. **Header at 375px or less** — PLAUSIBLE. `AppShell.tsx:23-46`: wordmark, `gap-x-6` and three `px-3` links come to roughly 345-370px against 343 available, so the nav likely wraps once the cart badge appears. Fix: `gap-x-3 sm:gap-x-6`, links `px-2 sm:px-3`.

14. **Cramped grids** — PLAUSIBLE. Deals `lg:grid-cols-6` (`HomeBlocks.tsx:140`) gives about 144px cards at 1024; Featured `sm:grid-cols-4` (`:185`) about 140px at 640. Each deal card also shows its discount twice with different minus glyphs (`HomeBlocks.tsx:145` "−", `PriceTag.tsx:35` "-").

15. **Small ones** — CONFIRMED unless noted.
   - Hero CTA `text-base` loses to `text-[15px]` in the built CSS (`HomeBlocks.tsx:68`).
   - Raw colour `accent-[#fad318]` (`OrderPage.tsx:220`) against `accent-accent` (`FilterPanel.tsx:75`).
   - Inputs use three backgrounds and two heights (`SearchBox.tsx:154`, `AccountPage.tsx:108`, `FilterPanel.tsx:101`, `StorePage.tsx:109`).
   - Region label is plain in `OrderLines.tsx:22`, bold uppercase elsewhere.
   - Breadcrumb is a `<nav>` at `ProductPage.tsx:72` and a `<p>` at `OrderPage.tsx:89`.
   - `<h3>` inside `<span>` (`HomeBlocks.tsx:116-118,170-171`).
   - A product with no offers gets a yellow "Compare 0 offers" button (`ProductPage.tsx:144-146`) — PLAUSIBLE, data-dependent.
   - Touch targets under 24px: Copy (`OrderPage.tsx:348`), Clear links (`StorePage.tsx:55,96`), "N in cart" (`OfferTable.tsx:72`).
   - No focus move or announcement on route change (`AppShell.tsx:50`).
   - A failed sign-out is silent (`AccountPage.tsx:39-46`).
   - No `onError` on any image.
   - Stale comment at `ProductCard.tsx:7`; unused type `CartIssue` (`checkout.ts:11`).

## Checked, fine

- **Routing:** 404 route inside the shell; `ScrollRestoration` present; `next` redirect accepts only same-site paths (`AccountPage.tsx:16`); `/orders` and `/orders/:id` handle 401 with a sign-in link; no lazy chunks to fail.
- **Security:** no `dangerouslySetInnerHTML`, no env or secrets in the bundle, CSRF header sent, keys never written to localStorage or logs (storage holds only cart, idempotency key and order id).
- **Focus and motion:** one global `:focus-visible` ring, never removed; reduced-motion handled (`index.css:34-44`).
- **Accessibility markup:** `lang="en"`, skip link, landmarks, labels on every input, combobox keyboard pattern, `aria-live` on the store count and search status, `aria-current` on nav and pagination.
- **Titles:** the document title changes on every route.
- **Prices and images:** all prices go through `formatMoney` and `PriceTag`; every `<img>` has width/height; covers are about 1 KB SVGs, so eager loading is reasonable; every cover in the contract fixtures exists in `public/covers`.
- **Leftovers:** no TODOs, `console.*`, "coming later" copy or dead components.
- **Queries:** home, store products, product, cart quote, orders, order and suggestions each have loading, error-with-retry and empty states (exceptions listed above); 4xx responses are not retried.
- **Offer table and cart lines at 375px:** tight but should not overflow (estimated from classes, not measured).
```
